## Summary
<h2>2026.10.10-beta.1</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Faster, steadier Gateway:</strong> chat turns, session lists, startup, and idle Gateways do far less SQLite and main-thread work, with more database work moved onto worker threads, so busy hosts and large agent fleets stay responsive. <li><strong>Newer Apple platforms required:</strong> OpenClaw now requires macOS 26.2, iOS 26.2, and Safari 26.2 or newer. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5794115867" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168595" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595">#168595</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Apple platform requirement:</strong> require macOS, iOS, and Safari 26.2 or newer for the native apps and Control UI. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5794115867" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168595" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595">#168595</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Cloud sessions:</strong> move sessions between local and cloud from session menus, organize cloud environments by repository, support repository environment recipes with verified Node toolchains, save suggested cloud profiles on first use, show snapshot startup progress, start sessions while the first snapshot uploads, ready workers for empty-workspace sessions, report slow dispatch phases, and keep cloud setup secrets behind scoped egress grants. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5841723580" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171690" <li><strong>Desktop apps and first run:</strong> support local Gateways in the Windows desktop app, start fresh Tauri installs with a local Gateway, start fresh macOS installs in guided dashboard setup, guide missing native permissions in chat, and choose inference automatically during first-run setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5807280480" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169522" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169522/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169522">#169522</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5807135173" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169512" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169512/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169512">#169512</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5807853256" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169568" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169568/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169568">#169568</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5808877988" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169635" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169635/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169635">#169635</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5808772768" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169628" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169628/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169628">#169628</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Local setup:</strong> offer optional memory embedding setup, add managed llama.cpp OCR and vision setup, and allow non-interactive llama.cpp embedding-only setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5681343582" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/163693" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163693/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163693">#163693</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5448380237" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/148193" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148193/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148193">#148193</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5819192610" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/170153" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170153/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170153">#170153</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/PollyBot13/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/PollyBot13">@PollyBot13</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/duyufansh2008/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/duyufansh2008">@duyufansh2008</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/dh-js/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/dh-js">@dh-js</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Bun runtime:</strong> build standalone OpenClaw executables with Bun, add an opt-in pinned Bun runtime that rejects unpinnable paths before changing anything, and start the Gateway faster on Bun. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5852780817" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/172384" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172384/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172384">#172384</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5797340670" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168832" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168832/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168832">#168832</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5799073394" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168982" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168982/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168982">#168982</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5807495671" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169543" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169543/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169543">#169543</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Experimental PostgreSQL:</strong> add an experimental PostgreSQL engine for the Workboard store, select the shared-state engine at its owner, generate PostgreSQL DDL from canonical SQLite schemas, add PostgreSQL migrations with capability admission and exact schema-drift checks, preserve PostgreSQL stores in backups and updates, and add verified offline database port tooling. <li><strong>Clock changes:</strong> keep Codex, Gateway dispatch, MCP loopback, SSH worker retry, cron repair, Comfy, Crabbox, and remote restart deadlines on the monotonic clock so wall-clock changes do not cut them short or stall them. (#155801, #168144, #168298, #168731, #168736, #168771, #170495, #171094, #172256, #171097, #162779, #169908, #168087) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/SunnyShu0925/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/SunnyShu0925">@SunnyShu0925</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, and @altaywtf.</li>
<li><strong>Streaming performance:</strong> keep the Gateway responsive during reply-tagged streams and stop long streamed runs and citation replies rescanning earlier text on every delta. (#166782, #171361, #172470) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/boeingchoco/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/boeingchoco">@boeingchoco</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/JasonBuildAI/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/JasonBuildAI">@JasonBuildAI</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Session and database performance:</strong> keep cold session and memory SQL off the Gateway main thread, move transcript bookkeeping and target lookups into workers, reuse committed facts and admission snapshots across turns, retire idle transcript workers, and avoid repeated schema checks after clean shutdowns. (#169164, #169553, #169757, #169960, #167739, #168647, #169649, #171038, #171155, #171354, #170017, #171883, #169052, #171322, #168975, #169110, #172372) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Patrick-Erichsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen">@Patrick-Erichsen</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Kimiyu-186/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Kimiyu-186">@Kimiyu-186</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/VACInc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/VACInc">@VACInc</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Startup and idle performance:</strong> listen before session projection, overlap startup preparations, admit agents before transcript repair, avoid database polling and maintenance wakeups in idle Gateways, park idle timers, and move fleet skill discovery and update schema publication off the main thread. (#170151, #169328, #170849, #169871, #170531, #171318, #170755, #170933, #171179, #169138, #170401, #171006, #171028, #171685, #170341) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/synthalorian/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/synthalorian">@synthalorian</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Turn and prompt performance:</strong> stabilize prompt prefixes across images, retries, tools, and runs, reuse tool descriptors and dynamic schemas between turns, initialize session tools once, and remove redundant streaming awaits. (#169091, #169237, #170050, #170282, #170516, #170610, #170673, #171924, #171874, #168848, #171696, #170185, #170262, #171574) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/joshavant/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/joshavant">@joshavant</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Control UI performance:</strong> reduce cold-load chat rendering, sidebar churn, layout, and style recalculation on reloads, shrink session list payloads, load filtered sidebar rows first, and avoid reconnecting on delayed heartbeats. (#169707, #169680, #169288, #170629, #170648, #170637, #170082, #169062, #171671, #171253, #170047, #170024, #171534, #171610) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Worktree performance:</strong> start first replies while checkouts finish, reuse templates and prepared bases, avoid eager partial-clone blobs, and bound repository housekeeping. (#169136, #169239, #169359, #169590, #168682, #171680, #169070, #171841, #171978)</li>
</ul>

<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Apple platform requirement:</strong> require macOS, iOS, and Safari 26.2 or newer for the native apps and Control UI. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5794115867" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/168595" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168595">#168595</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Cloud sessions:</strong> move sessions between local and cloud from session menus, organize cloud environments by repository, support repository environment recipes with verified Node toolchains, save suggested cloud profiles on first use, show snapshot startup progress, start sessions while the first snapshot uploads, ready workers for empty-workspace sessions, report slow dispatch phases, and keep cloud setup secrets behind scoped egress grants. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5841723580" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171690" <li><strong>Desktop apps and first run:</strong> support local Gateways in the Windows desktop app, start fresh Tauri installs with a local Gateway, start fresh macOS installs in guided dashboard setup, guide missing native permissions in chat, and choose inference automatically during first-run setup.
